SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Orders] AS [o0]
    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s3].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s3] ON [s2].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s3].[Id]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]

SELECT [o].[Id] AS [OwnerId], [o].[Name], [o].[Phone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
    WHERE [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]) AS [RestaurantCount], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) AS [ActiveSubscriptionCount], (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM([s0].[PricePaid]), 0.0)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
    WHERE [s0].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s0].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSpent], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[PurchaseDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s1].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [s1].[PurchaseDate] DESC) AS [LastPurchaseDate]
FROM [Owners] AS [o]

    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]

SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Orders] AS [o0]
    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s3].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s3] ON [s2].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s3].[Id]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]

        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'

    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Orders] AS [o0]
    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s3].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]

    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Orders] AS [o0]
    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (

    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]

SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Orders] AS [o0]
    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]

    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]

    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (

SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Orders] AS [o0]
    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s3].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s3] ON [s2].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s3].[Id]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]

SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Orders] AS [o0]
    WHERE [o0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_0) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s3].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s3] ON [s2].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s3].[Id]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]

    WHERE [o1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (

    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]

)

SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Orders] AS [o1]
    WHERE [o1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) AS [TotalOrders], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s3].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s3] ON [s2].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s3].[Id]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [o0].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__ownerIdsToExclude_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [o0]
)

        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
        WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s1].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (

    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s3] ON [s2].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s3].[Id]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]

    FROM OPENJSON(@__ownerIdsToExclude_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [o0]
)

SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSubscriptions], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s2].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s4].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s3]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s4] ON [s3].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s4].[Id]
    WHERE [s3].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s3].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)

    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]

INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s0] ON [s].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s0].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)
ORDER BY [s].[PurchaseDate] DESC

    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSubscriptions], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s2].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]

    WHERE [s3].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s3].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]

    WHEN EXISTS (

        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'

2026-06-19 19:31:22.779 +03:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (88ms) [Parameters=[@__today_1='?' (DbType = DateTime2), @__excludedIds_0='?' (Size = 4000)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSubscriptions], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s2].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s4].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s3]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s4] ON [s3].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s4].[Id]
    WHERE [s3].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s3].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)

    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
    WHERE [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]) AS [RestaurantCount], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) AS [ActiveSubscriptionCount], (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM([s0].[PricePaid]), 0.0)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
    WHERE [s0].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s0].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSpent], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[PurchaseDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s1].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [s1].[PurchaseDate] DESC) AS [LastPurchaseDate]
FROM [Owners] AS [o]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]

SELECT [s].[Id], [r].[name] AS [RestaurantName], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [s0].[Name] AS [PlanName], [s].[EndDate]
FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
INNER JOIN [Restaurants] AS [r] ON [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [s].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id]
INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s0] ON [s].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s0].[Id]

2026-06-19 19:33:06.615 +03:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (86ms) [Parameters=[@__today_1='?' (DbType = DateTime2), @__excludedIds_0='?' (Size = 4000)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSubscriptions], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s2].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (

SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSubscriptions], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s2].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s4].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s3]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s4] ON [s3].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s4].[Id]
    WHERE [s3].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s3].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)

SELECT [o].[Id] AS [OwnerId], [o].[Name], [o].[Phone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
    WHERE [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]) AS [RestaurantCount], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) AS [ActiveSubscriptionCount], (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM([s0].[PricePaid]), 0.0)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
    WHERE [s0].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s0].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSpent], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[PurchaseDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s1].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [s1].[PurchaseDate] DESC) AS [LastPurchaseDate]
FROM [Owners] AS [o]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)

2026-06-19 19:46:44.717 +03:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (76ms) [Parameters=[@__today_1='?' (DbType = DateTime2), @__excludedIds_0='?' (Size = 4000)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSubscriptions], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s2].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s4].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s3]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s4] ON [s3].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s4].[Id]
    WHERE [s3].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s3].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)

    FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]

    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) AS [ActiveSubscriptionCount], (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM([s0].[PricePaid]), 0.0)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
    WHERE [s0].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s0].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSpent], (

    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s1].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)

FROM [Owners] AS [o]

2026-06-19 19:52:05.448 +03:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (123ms) [Parameters=[@__today_1='?' (DbType = DateTime2), @__excludedIds_0='?' (Size = 4000)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSubscriptions], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s2].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s4].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s3]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s4] ON [s3].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s4].[Id]
    WHERE [s3].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s3].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)

2026-06-19 19:56:35.210 +03:30 [INF] Executed DbCommand (90ms) [Parameters=[@__today_1='?' (DbType = DateTime2), @__excludedIds_0='?' (Size = 4000)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT [r].[restaurant_id] AS [RestaurantId], [r].[name] AS [Name], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [o].[Phone] AS [OwnerPhone], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSubscriptions], CASE
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
        WHERE [s0].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id] AND [s0].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s0].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) THEN N'Active'
    WHEN EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
        WHERE [s1].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]) THEN N'Expired'
    ELSE N'None'
END AS [SubscriptionStatus], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s2].[EndDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s2]
    WHERE [s2].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s2].[EndDate] DESC) AS [SubscriptionEndDate], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s4].[Name]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s3]
    INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s4] ON [s3].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s4].[Id]
    WHERE [s3].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
    ORDER BY [s3].[EndDate] DESC) AS [PlanName]
FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)

    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Restaurants] AS [r]
    WHERE [r].[owner_id] = [o].[Id]) AS [RestaurantCount], (
    SELECT COUNT(*)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
    WHERE [s].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s].[Status] = N'Active' AND [s].[EndDate] >= @__today_1) AS [ActiveSubscriptionCount], (
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM([s0].[PricePaid]), 0.0)
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s0]
    WHERE [s0].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s0].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)) AS [TotalSpent], (
    SELECT TOP(1) [s1].[PurchaseDate]
    FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s1]
    WHERE [s1].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id] AND [s1].[IsPaid] = CAST(1 AS bit)
    ORDER BY [s1].[PurchaseDate] DESC) AS [LastPurchaseDate]
FROM [Owners] AS [o]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]

SELECT TOP(@__p_1) [s].[Id] AS [SubscriptionId], [r].[name] AS [RestaurantName], [o].[Name] AS [OwnerName], [s0].[Name] AS [PlanName], [s].[PricePaid], [s].[PurchaseDate], [s].[Status]
FROM [Subscriptions] AS [s]
INNER JOIN [Restaurants] AS [r] ON [s].[RestaurantId] = [r].[restaurant_id]
INNER JOIN [Owners] AS [o] ON [s].[OwnerId] = [o].[Id]
INNER JOIN [SubscriptionPlans] AS [s0] ON [s].[SubscriptionPlanId] = [s0].[Id]
WHERE [o].[Id] NOT IN (
    SELECT [e].[value]
    FROM OPENJSON(@__excludedIds_0) WITH ([value] int '$') AS [e]
)
ORDER BY [s].[PurchaseDate] DESC
